First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [169] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出针对脉冲神经网络与神经形态事件数据的清洁标签时序投毒攻击：仅改变目标类别训练流的时间戳而不改标签，并保持每个像素、极性的事件总数不变。该攻击在强配置下达到1.00攻击成功率，同时揭示时间聚合型防御的固有盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN后门研究主要依赖脏标签投毒，即将带触发器样本重标为攻击者指定类别。这种设定较易暴露，且未充分利用事件数据的时间维度。论文旨在研究一种不修改标签、仅操纵事件时序的攻击方式，并评估现有防御面对时序隐蔽触发器时的有效性。

Method: 作者对目标类别训练样本施加固定时间戳变换，标签保持原样。变换严格保留逐像素、逐极性的事件计数，因此将事件流沿时间轴聚合后，原样本与触发样本不可区分；但SNN实际处理的时序序列已发生变化。实验覆盖三个神经形态数据集，以及卷积式和Transformer式SNN受害模型。作者进一步进行投毒比例与触发器形状消融，并测试适配SNN的既有后门防御；同时提出无模型检测器，以各时间步事件量分布作为检测依据。

Result: 在最强实验配置中，攻击成功率达到1.00。消融实验用于刻画投毒预算和不同时间触发形状对攻击效果的影响。先将时间轴压缩或聚合再检查的防御方法由于丢失时序信息而在机制上无法识别该攻击；特征空间方法仅在部分设置中有效。作者提出的基于逐时间步事件量的检测器能够识别所评估的时间戳变换，说明该攻击并非对所有时序统计检测都完全隐蔽。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将清洁标签后门威胁扩展到SNN事件流的纯时序操纵场景，并表明仅依赖事件总量或时间聚合表示的防御不足以应对此类攻击。防御设计应保留并审查细粒度时间结构；同时，基于时间步事件质量的简单统计检测可构成有效补充。摘要中的“首次”主张应结合完整文献综述进一步核验。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [170] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Agent Harness Distillation（AHD）框架，研究攻击者能否仅通过黑盒交互提取基于大语言模型的自主多智能体系统在推理时动态形成的协作编排机制，并提出相应防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时编排机制承担多智能体系统中的角色协调、推理流程与行动组织，通常需要大量工程调优和算力投入，具有较高知识产权价值。现有研究主要关注静态、预配置多智能体架构的泄露问题，尚未系统评估动态推理时编排机制是否也可被外部交互复制。

Method: 作者将“编排机制提取”形式化为新的安全问题，并构建风险评估框架。AHD包含两个阶段：预蒸馏阶段从目标智能体的输出中推断其推理时行为模式，并据此构建初始编排机制；后蒸馏阶段则通过迭代优化，使初始机制在行为上进一步贴近目标智能体。论文还设计了基于欺骗的防御方法，旨在降低提取成功率，同时维持受保护智能体的任务效用。

Result: 在多个真实世界自主多智能体系统及不同底座大语言模型上的实验表明，AHD能够有效复现目标系统的部分推理时编排能力，说明该类系统存在显著的知识产权泄露风险。摘要未提供具体任务指标、提取成本、成功率或防御带来的效用损失数值。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是揭示动态推理时编排机制同样可能成为可被黑盒蒸馏和复制的资产，并提供了攻击建模、评估流程与初步防御思路。其意义在于将多智能体系统的保护边界从模型参数和静态架构扩展到推理阶段的行为编排；后续应进一步验证该方法在不同任务、访问预算和更强防御条件下的泛化性。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks. AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [171] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统研究面向持续音频交互多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入风险，提出攻击方法、评测基准与防御机制，并报告了较高攻击成功率及超过90%的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续音频交互使智能体能够自然地接收用户语音与环境声音，但环境音频可能夹带非用户可控的指令。现有研究主要关注传统语音设备的声学攻击，尚未充分覆盖多模态智能体在并发语音场景下的指令注入、隐蔽性和防护问题。

Method: 作者提出两类攻击技术：指令增强用于提升注入指令的可执行性，场景隐藏用于降低恶意指令在用户语音中的可感知性。随后构建AudioAgentSecurity基准，覆盖8类真实任务场景和10种攻击模式，并评测11个主流智能体。防御方面提出CADV，通过声源分离将潜在指令与用户语音解耦，再利用跨模态一致性分析识别异常音频指令。

Result: 在所述实验中，攻击方法针对Gemini 3 Pro取得69.10%的平均攻击成功率。CADV在多类攻击向量上实现超过90%的检测成功率。作者还在真实动态环境下的手机智能体交互实验中验证了攻击的隐蔽性与有效性，并显示CADV能够缓解相关风险。

Conclusion: 论文表明，连续音频输入是多模态智能体的重要安全边界，单纯依赖文本级提示防护可能不足。基于声源分离和跨模态一致性验证的CADV提供了更贴合音频媒介特性的防护思路。其主要贡献在于同时给出攻击范式、标准化评测基准和防御方案；后续仍需验证其在更多语言、设备、噪声条件及复杂人机对话中的泛化性、误报率与实时开销。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [172] [Secure Aggregation for Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning on Clinical EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28191)
*Pouya Rajabi,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向临床脑电图数据的隐私保护联邦学习框架，以基于掩码的安全聚合为核心，在不共享原始数据的前提下进一步隐藏单个客户端的模型更新，并比较半诚实、恶意安全和辅助公证三类配置的安全性与开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习虽然避免直接交换原始临床脑电图数据，但服务器或其他参与方仍可能通过个体模型更新推断隐私信息。医疗跨机构协作还面临客户端掉线、参与方可信度有限、数据记录关联及结果可验证性等实际问题，因此需要具备隐私、鲁棒性和可审计性的安全聚合机制。

Method: 框架采用掩码式安全聚合，并整合图结构通信、门限秘密共享和抗掉线聚合，以保障客户端更新在聚合过程中的机密性与可恢复性；使用本地更新裁剪限制异常更新影响；可选地通过基于布隆过滤器的隐私保护记录链接模块完成初始化；引入辅助公证方支持轻量级验证。方案覆盖半诚实与恶意聚合场景，并基于Flower实现，在模拟的跨机构医疗联邦学习环境中、使用TUH EEG衍生数据和不同客户端配置进行评估。

Result: 在论文给定的威胁模型和假设下，安全聚合变体能够防止聚合服务器直接获知单个客户端更新，并且仍可支持联邦模型训练。半诚实安全版本在安全配置中具有最低计算、通信和单轮训练时延开销。面向恶意参与方的版本及辅助公证版本通过一致性检查、完整性保障和轻量验证提升安全能力，但会带来额外计算、通信负担和更长的训练轮次时间。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，掩码式安全聚合可以较好地嵌入临床脑电图联邦学习流程，在训练可用性与更新隐私之间取得平衡。实际部署应根据威胁强度选择配置：若主要防范诚实但好奇的服务器，可采用低开销半诚实方案；若需抵御恶意行为或增强完整性与可验证性，则应接受更高系统成本。摘要未提供具体性能指标、模型精度变化及大规模真实部署结果，这些仍是判断其实用性的关键证据。

Abstract: Federated learning enables multiple institutions to train shared models without exchanging raw clinical EEG data, but it does not fully prevent privacy leakage from individual model updates. This paper presents a privacy-preserving federated learning framework for clinical EEG data using masking-based secure aggregation as the core protection mechanism. The framework combines graph-based communication, threshold secret sharing, dropout-resilient aggregation, local update clipping, an optional Bloom filter-based privacy-preserving record-linkage initialization module, and auxiliary-notary-based verifiability. It supports both semi-honest and malicious aggregation settings and is implemented using the Flower federated learning framework. The secure-aggregation variants are evaluated in a simulated cross-silo healthcare setting using TUH EEG-derived data under different client configurations. Under the stated assumptions, the secure variants hide individual updates from the aggregation server. The results show that these variants remain compatible with federated model training, although malicious-setting safeguards and lightweight consistency-checking mechanisms introduce additional computation, communication, and round-duration overhead. The semi-honest variant provides the lowest overhead among the secure configurations, while malicious and auxiliary-notary variants offer stronger consistency, integrity, and lightweight verification support at higher cost.

</details>


### [173] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该摘要是一篇关于具身人工智能世界模型安全性的综述，主张将安全分析覆盖从数据到部署和长期适应的完整生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通过预测未来状态支持规划与控制，但其预测能力也扩大了攻击面：数据、传感器、提示、反馈或供应链中的污染均可能经由错误的状态表征、动力学预测或安全代价估计传导至实际执行行为。

Method: 论文构建生命周期安全分类法，覆盖数据构建、表征学习、状态落地、想象预测、轨迹评估、执行、记忆与工具驱动的长期适应；并将数据投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵和供应链攻击映射到世界模型的安全属性。同时提出围绕数据来源、鲁棒状态落地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障的防御框架，以及用于评测安全失效的协议。

Result: 摘要的核心发现是，传统攻击在世界模型场景中具有新的失效机制：它们可篡改世界状态、学习到的环境动力学、可供性估计或安全成本。论文还指出双重效应：世界模型能够充当运行时安全屏障，但一旦被攻破或被过度信任，也可能制造具有误导性的“预测安全幻觉”。

Conclusion: 该综述强调，世界模型安全不能仅视为单一模型鲁棒性问题，而应被作为端到端系统安全问题处理。有效保障需同时控制训练数据与组件来源、感知和状态对齐、预测不确定性、规划轨迹、在线反馈及部署链路。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [CircuitProver: Agentic Lean 4 Theorem Proving with Reusable Circuit Proof Library for Hardware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27259)
*Ziyi Yang,Wenji Fang,Chen Chen,Zhiyao Xie,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出CircuitProver：一个基于Lean 4的智能体硬件形式化验证框架，通过将参数化硬件设计及自然语言规格转化为可执行Lean模型，自动构造可机检证明，并沉淀可复用的证明策略与定理库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成电路复杂度提升使功能验证成为瓶颈。主流模型检查通常对每个设计实例独立验证，结果主要是通过或失败，证明过程难以解释和复用；交互式定理证明虽可生成显式证明工件，但硬件场景下仍高度依赖专家进行建模、不变量发现和证明开发。因此，需要能自动化、可积累且支持参数化设计验证的方法。

Method: CircuitProver采用Lean 4作为证明内核，自动将参数化硬件代码和自然语言规格翻译为Lean中的可执行模型；智能体根据Lean反馈迭代生成、修正和验证证明。系统将证明轨迹、验证定理及证明策略蒸馏为可复用库：策略用于指导后续智能体推理，已验证引理用于相似硬件任务中的形式化复用。论文还构建了面向智能体硬件定理证明的基准套件，覆盖不同参数化设计、规格、证明任务与评估指标。

Result: 在63个基准任务上，CircuitProver完成全部证明任务。相比之下，未使用知识积累机制的基础智能体完成率为92.1%，且平均需要约两倍证明轮次。消融实验表明，积累的证明知识能够减少相关任务中的重复证明构造，使证明长度降低16.3%，验证时间降低23.2%。

Conclusion: CircuitProver表明，结合Lean 4机检证明、智能体迭代推理与跨任务知识积累，可有效提升参数化硬件形式化验证的自动化程度、证明复用性和验证效率。其贡献包括可复用证明工件机制、面向参数化硬件的自动证明流程，以及首个用于评估智能体硬件定理证明的基准套件。

Abstract: Modern integrated circuits (ICs) are becoming increasingly complex, making functional verification a major bottleneck. The dominant hardware formal verification methodology, model checking, verifies each design instance separately and exposes only pass/fail results, so the reasoning behind a proof stays locked inside solver heuristics and is repeatedly reconstructed across related designs. Interactive theorem proving instead yields explicit, reusable proof artifacts, but applying it to hardware remains largely manual, demanding expert effort for formalization, invariant discovery, and proof development. In this paper, we present CircuitProver, an agentic Lean 4-based verification framework supporting proof-accumulation and parameterized verification. CircuitProver automatically translates parameterized hardware designs and their natural language specifications into executable Lean 4 models. It then iteratively constructs machine-checked proofs through Lean feedback to establish that the hardware code complies with the specification. The proving traces and verified theorems are distilled into reusable libraries, where proving strategies guide future agent reasoning and verified lemmas support formal proof reuse across related hardware verification tasks. We further introduce the first benchmark suite for evaluating agentic hardware theorem proving, covering diverse parameterized hardware designs, specifications, proof tasks, and evaluation metrics. Across 63 tasks, CircuitProver successfully proves all benchmarks, while a vanilla agent solves 92.1% of them and requires twice as many proof rounds on average. Ablation studies show that accumulated proof knowledge reduces redundant proof construction across related verification tasks, reducing proof length by 16.3% and verification time by 23.2%.

</details>


### [175] [From Lecture Notes to Lean: Formalizing a Textbook on Probability Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27298)
*Shuo Deng,Kenneth W. Shum*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文报告了对一本十四章测度论概率教材的持续 Lean 形式化工作，目标是构建经机器验证的教材配套版本，并沉淀可复用的概率论形式化基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型可生成大量看似合理的数学论证，数学工作的重点将更多转向证明验证、清晰阐释及结果的可复用整合。教材形式化可使定理陈述、假设和证明逻辑接受严格检查，并服务教学与可靠的 AI 辅助数学。

Method: 项目将教材中的内容逐步编码为 Lean 定理和证明，覆盖从 Riemann--Stieltjes 积分到鞅与极限定理的主题。方法上优先复用 Mathlib 中已有的测度论结果；当教材表述与 Mathlib 的通用抽象接口不一致时，补充可审查的接口引理，以连接面向教材的结论与库中的一般化结构。

Result: 当前产出包括教材结果的机器检查版本，以及面向后续概率论形式化任务的可复用基础设施。项目同时展示了如何将教材中的隐含条件显式化，并让读者能够检查每个结果的精确逻辑内容。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，Lean 形式化教材不仅可提升数学内容的可验证性和教学透明度，也能通过接口引理与库复用机制积累长期可维护的形式化基础，为可靠的 AI 辅助数学研究提供支撑。

Abstract: As large language models become increasingly capable of generating mathematical arguments, mathematics is likely to face not a scarcity of proofs but an abundance of plausible ones. In such an environment, verification, exposition, and incorporation into reusable mathematical infrastructure become central tasks. We report on an ongoing Lean formalization of "Measure-Theoretic Probability: With Applications to Statistics, Finance, and Engineering", a fourteen-chapter upper-level undergraduate textbook covering topics from Riemann--Stieltjes integration to martingales and limit theorems.
  The project produces a machine-checked companion to the textbook and contributes reusable infrastructure for future formalizations involving probability theory. A Lean formalization provides computer-checked statements and proofs, makes hypotheses explicit, and allows readers to inspect the precise logical content of textbook results. A central challenge is to bridge textbook-facing statements with Mathlib's more general measure-theoretic interfaces. We reuse Mathlib results when possible and introduce reviewable interface lemmas when the textbook formulation and library abstraction differ. The project illustrates how formalized textbooks can support teaching, clarify mathematical assumptions, and help build the formal foundations needed for reliable AI-assisted mathematics.

</details>


### [176] [Extension Types for Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27387)
*Nicolai Kraus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文在二层类型论（2LTT）中统一刻画扩展类型（extension types），证明其可定义且可严格满足既有扩展类型演算规则；该框架相对同伦类型论（HoTT）保守，并用于建立立方粘合与单价性之间的等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径类型、子形状上的名称赋值扩展类型，以及受控展开机制都表达了“项在边界或条件下被部分固定”的需求，但通常依赖额外的面演算或形状层。作者旨在给出一个无需新增公理或专门模型构造的统一基础，并借此比较不同类型论系统。

Method: 作者以2LTT为基础，利用其严格层与同伦层的结合定义扩展类型；证明标准HoTT模型自动给出2LTT模型，且2LTT对HoTT保守。随后在该定义下验证扩展类型的严格规则，推导相对函数外延性等原先需假设的性质。最后以该框架分析立方粘合，证明其适当形式与单价性等价，并在Agda--two-level中自动形式化主要结果。

Result: 扩展类型在2LTT中可定义，Riehl和Shulman的完整扩展类型演算得到严格验证，相关公理可转化为定理。任意HoTT基础模型都可扩展为带扩展类型的同一理论模型，核心额外选择仅为指定哪些映射属于余纤维化（cofibrations）。此外，论文证明适当表述的立方粘合等价于单价性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明扩展类型不必作为独立原语或依赖专门语义模型，而可由保守的2LTT框架统一实现。其结果为比较立方类型论与传统HoTT提供了工具，并提出了证明立方类型论相对书本HoTT保守的一条潜在路线。

Abstract: Extension types are a concept in dependent type theory that has appeared in various contexts. The idea is to have types whose terms are partially determined, e.g. via a strict boundary condition. Standard examples are path types of cubical type theories (paths with fixed endpoints), Riehl and Shulman's name-giving extension types (terms fixed on subshapes), as well as the controlled-unfolding mechanism of cooltt and Agda (terms that are fixed if a condition is met). In each case, the type theory is equipped with a (meta-theoretic) face calculus, or shape layer, that governs their rules, and comes with intended semantics.
  We unify all these occurrences in a single framework where no new axioms or model constructions are needed, namely two-level type theory. This step, too, is free (semantically): the standard models of HoTT are automatically models of 2LTT, and the theory is conservative over HoTT. Extension types are definable, and the definition validates Riehl and Shulman's entire extension-type calculus: the rules hold strictly, and the postulated axioms, such as relative function extensionality, become theorems. In this way, every model of the base theory (HoTT) gives rise to a model of the same theory with extension types; the only genuine assumptions are which maps count as cofibrations.
  Conservativity makes the framework a tool for comparing type theories. We prove that cubical gluing, in a suitable formulation, is equivalent to univalence. On this basis, we suggest an approach toward the conjecture that cubical type theories are conservative over book HoTT, one of the central open problems of homotopy type theory.
  All results of the main body of the paper are auto-formalized in Agda --two-level, in a development that combines HoTT-internal arguments with reasoning that is external to HoTT.

</details>


### [177] [Some Experiments with Twee-Style Goal-Directedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27442)
*Stephan Schulz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向饱和式一阶定理证明的子句选择方法：优先处理与待证目标共享项的子句，并给出无需通过等式定义改写问题的替代实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在饱和式定理证明中，下一条待处理子句的选择会显著影响搜索效率。已有系统Twee通过引入等式定义，使与猜想共享项的子句获得优先级；该工作旨在将这一有效思想推广到完整一阶逻辑，并探索更直接的实现方式。

Method: 作者以子句和猜想之间的共享项为依据设计选择启发式，在完整一阶逻辑证明场景中应用该原则。与Twee依赖添加等式定义来变换原问题的方法不同，论文提出了基于共享项的替代性实现。

Result: 摘要称，该替代实现取得了“非常有前景”的结果，表明共享项导向的子句选择能够改善一阶定理证明中的搜索表现。摘要未提供具体基准、成功率、运行时间或与现有方法的量化对比。

Conclusion: 共享项，尤其是与待证目标共享的项，可作为有效的子句选择信号。该论文的主要贡献是将该思路扩展到完整一阶逻辑，并提出一种更直接的实现方案；其实际优势仍需结合正文中的实验设计和量化结果进一步评估。

Abstract: In saturation-based theorem proving, selecting the next clause for processing is a major concern. Twee has successfully applied the idea of preferring clauses that share terms with the conjecture by adding equational definitions to transform the problem. In this paper, we apply the idea to the full first-order case, and offer an alternative implementation based on shared terms that shows very promising results.

</details>


### [178] [Certified Sequential Sweep Without Unrolling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27498)
*Tobias Seufert,Christoph Scholl*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于IC3的时序等价验证方法，用于验证经过重定时及后续强序列重综合优化后的电路，并能生成验证证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序等价检查工具通常依赖内部重综合及多个模型检查器的复杂协同，流程易出错且难以认证，因而限制了激进重定时和序列优化的实际应用。

Method: 该方法先将重定时作为预处理步骤，再通过仿真生成候选不变式，并采用IC3验证重定时前后及任意强度序列重综合后的时序等价性；验证过程同时支持证书生成。

Result: 在一组经过重定时和重综合的开放电路设计上，该方法显著优于最新硬件模型检查竞赛获胜工具所代表的通用可认证模型检查组合方案。与ABC等成熟但不可认证的等价检查方法相比，该方法仍具有竞争力，并在证书生成等方面具备互补优势。

Conclusion: 通过“重定时预处理、仿真候选不变式和IC3证明”的组合，论文实现了高效且可认证的序列等价验证，为采用激进重定时和序列重综合优化提供了更可靠的验证手段。

Abstract: Verifying retiming followed by additional sequential resynthesis steps remains challenging for existing tools, limiting aggressive optimizations. Commonly-used equivalence checking tools rely on internal resynthesis operations and error-prone or- chestration of different model checkers, which makes certification difficult or even infeasible. We present an IC3-based technique that uses retiming as a preprocessing step and uses simulation to generate suspected invariants. Our technique efficiently verifies sequential equivalence problems under retiming and arbitrarily strong sequential resynthesis, and has the additional feature of producing certificates all the same. Our results on a selection of retimed and resynthesized open circuit designs show that our rather simple approach vastly outperforms the whole portfolio of the winner of the latest Hardware Model Checking Competition as a representative of general-purpose certifying model checkers. Compared to non- certifying approaches, like the mature equivalence checker of ABC, we are still competitive with additional complementary strengths.

</details>
